        {
            "id": "19ea9800-47f8-4412-801c-af069ebd4349",
            "author": {
                "role": "assistant",
                "name": null,
                "metadata": {}
            },
            "create_time": 1791722603.482414,
            "update_time": 1791722613.060211,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "For your requirements, I’d start with **Arihant Abode and Coco County in Sector 10, Greater Noida West**. Both have 3BHK resale options, and this area has schools, residential green spaces and shopping destinations within the wider Noida Extension neighbourhood.\n\nSince you want ample open space, I would prioritise a flat overlooking a landscaped garden, a society with usable outdoor areas, and good separation between towers rather than simply choosing the largest apartment.\n\n## My first shortlist\n\n<box gap={4}>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Arihant Abode Sector 10 Greater Noida West residential towers landscaped green garden\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" width=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">1. <Entity category=\"point_of_interest\" value=\"Arihant Abode\" location=\"Sector 10, Greater Noida West\"/></title>\n      <badge color=\"success\">Best starting point</badge>\n      <title size=\"xl\" color=\"success\">₹1.10–1.37 Cr</title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Indicative advertised project price range; verify current resale listings.</text>\n      - **3BHK:** approximately 1,160–1,270 sq ft.\n      - **Open space:** landscaped areas, jogging track and community amenities.\n      - **Schools:** St. Teresa's School is listed as a nearby option.\n      - **Mall:** Gaur City Mall is advertised at roughly an 8-minute drive, depending on traffic. <Cite refs={[\"turn639189search2\",\"turn639189search3\",\"turn639189search4\"]}/>\n      <text><icon name=\"external-link\" size=\"xs\" inline/> [View 3BHK listings](https://www.magicbricks.com/project-arihant-abode-3bhk-for-sale-in-greater-noida-pppfs)</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Coco County Sector 10 Greater Noida West apartment society landscaped gardens residential complex\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" width=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">2. <Entity category=\"point_of_interest\" value=\"Coco County\" location=\"Sector 10, Greater Noida West\"/></title>\n      <title size=\"xl\">Around ₹1.25 Cr</title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">An advertised resale example, not a confirmed live offer.</text>\n      - **3BHK:** a listed unit was approximately 1,152 sq ft built-up.\n      - **Open space:** inspect the actual garden area, tower spacing and apartment's view.\n      - **Schools and shopping:** compare the route to your preferred school and Gaur City Mall before deciding. <Cite refs={[\"turn639189search0\",\"turn639189search1\"]}/>\n      <text><icon name=\"external-link\" size=\"xs\" inline/> [View 3BHK listings](https://www.nobroker.in/3bhk-flats-for-sale-in-ats-happy-trails-sector-10-noida-prjtl)</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"ATS Homekraft Happy Trails Sector 10 Greater Noida West residential apartment complex landscaped courtyard\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" width=\"124px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      <title size=\"lg\">3. <Entity ref=\"turn639189business4\" value=\"ATS HomeKraft Happy Trails\"/></title>\n      <title size=\"xl\">₹1.75–2.60 Cr</title>\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Indicative advertised 3BHK range; selected configurations and resale prices vary.</text>\n      - A larger-price-band alternative worth comparing for its residential environment and amenities.\n      - **Budget caution:** some advertised units may exceed ₹2 Cr.\n      - Verify the available unit's usable area, open view, school commute and final all-inclusive cost. <Cite refs={[\"turn639189search0\",\"turn639189search1\"]}/>\n      <text><icon name=\"external-link\" size=\"xs\" inline/> [View 3BHK listings](https://www.nobroker.in/3bhk-flats-for-sale-in-ats-happy-trails-sector-10-greater-noida-prjtl)</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## Nearby places to check\n\n<box gap={3}>\n  <row align=center gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"St Teresa's School Greater Noida West school building\" aspectRatio=\"1:1\" width=\"76px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **<Entity category=\"point_of_interest\" value=\"St. Teresa's School\" location=\"Greater Noida West\"/>**\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">A school to investigate near Arihant Abode. Confirm the exact commute and current admissions.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <row align=center gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Gaur City Mall Greater Noida West exterior shopping mall\" aspectRatio=\"1:1\" width=\"76px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **<Entity ref=\"turn639189business1\" value=\"Gaur City Mall\"/>**\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">A major shopping and entertainment destination in Greater Noida West.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <row align=center gap={3}>\n    <AsyncImage query=\"Greater Noida West landscaped residential green park walking paths\" aspectRatio=\"1:1\" width=\"76px\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **Green space and outdoor recreation**\n      <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Inspect the society's internal gardens and nearby parks in person; a green view from the balcony matters more than a project brochure's total area.</text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n<MapWidgetV2 title=\"Sector 10 property shortlist\" points={[{\"id\":\"arihant\",\"name\":\"Arihant Abode\",\"address\":\"Arihant Abode, GH4B, Sector 10, Vaidpura, Greater Noida, Uttar Pradesh 203207\"},{\"ref\":\"turn639189business4\"},{\"id\":\"coco\",\"name\":\"Coco County\",\"address\":\"Coco County, Sector 10, Greater Noida West, Uttar Pradesh\"},{\"ref\":\"turn639189business1\"},{\"ref\":\"turn639189business0\"}]} show_route={false} carousel={{\"visibility\":\"hidden\"}}/>\n\n## One thing I need to narrow this down\n\nI have your earlier preference for a **self-use property with a budget of up to ₹2 crore** in mind. Which of these matters most to you?\n\n{@body const [budget,setBudget] = DIL.useState(\"1.2–1.5 Cr\")}\n{@body const [size,setSize] = DIL.useState(\"1400+ sq ft\")}\n{@body const [possession,setPossession] = DIL.useState(\"Ready to move\")}\n<box border radius=\"xl\" padding={4} gap={4}>\n  <text weight=\"medium\">1. What's your preferred budget?</text>\n  <radio-group value={budget} onChange={setBudget} direction=\"col\">\n    <radio value=\"Under ₹1.2 Cr\">Under ₹1.2 crore</radio>\n    <radio value=\"1.2–1.5 Cr\">₹1.2–1.5 crore</radio>\n    <radio value=\"1.5–2 Cr\">₹1.5–2 crore</radio>\n    <radio value=\"Up to ₹2 Cr including all charges\">₹2 crore including registration and other charges</radio>\n  </radio-group>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <text weight=\"medium\">2. How spacious should the flat be?</text>\n  <radio-group value={size} onChange={setSize} direction=\"col\">\n    <radio value=\"1100–1300 sq ft\">1,100–1,300 sq ft</radio>\n    <radio value=\"1300–1400 sq ft\">1,300–1,400 sq ft</radio>\n    <radio value=\"1400+ sq ft\">1,400+ sq ft</radio>\n    <radio value=\"Maximum open space, even if flat is smaller\">Prioritise open space over flat size</radio>\n  </radio-group>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <text weight=\"medium\">3. When do you want to move in?</text>\n  <radio-group value={possession} onChange={setPossession} direction=\"col\">\n    <radio value=\"Ready to move\">Ready to move</radio>\n    <radio value=\"Within 1 year\">Within one year</radio>\n    <radio value=\"Under construction is fine\">Under construction is fine</radio>\n  </radio-group>\n  <button block onClick={() => GenUI.issueNewTurn(\"Find specific 3BHK flats for self-use in official Sector 10, Greater Noida West. Budget: \"+budget+\". Preferred size: \"+size+\". Possession: \"+possession+\". Prioritise ample usable green/open space, nearby schools, and a nearby mall. Provide current listings, prices, area, exact project location, and links. Keep Sector 10 as a hard location filter.\")}>Find matching flats</button>\n</box>\n\nOnce these are set, I can narrow the search to actual available units rather than just residential projects."
                ]
            },
            "status": "finished_successfully",
            "end_turn": true,

                    "fallbackMarkdown": "For your requirements, I’d start with **Arihant Abode and Coco County in Sector 10, Greater Noida West**\\. Both have 3BHK resale options, and this area has schools, residential green spaces and shopping destinations within the wider Noida Extension neighbourhood.\n\nSince you want ample open space, I would prioritise a flat overlooking a landscaped garden, a society with usable outdoor areas, and good separation between towers rather than simply choosing the largest apartment.\n\n## My first shortlist\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Arihant Abode Sector 10 Greater Noida West residential towers landscaped green garden\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"3:4\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 1\\. entity[\"point_of_interest\",\"Arihant Abode\",null,{\"location\":\"Sector 10, Greater Noida West\"}]\n\n(Best starting point)\n\n# ₹1.10–1.37 Cr\n\nIndicative advertised project price range; verify current resale listings.\n\n- **3BHK\\:** approximately 1,160–1,270 sq ft.\n- **Open space\\:** landscaped areas, jogging track and community amenities.\n- **Schools\\:** St. Teresa's School is listed as a nearby option.\n- **Mall\\:** Gaur City Mall is advertised at roughly an 8-minute drive, depending on traffic. citeturn639189search2turn639189search3turn639189search4\n  \n  urlView 3BHK listingshttps://www.magicbricks.com/project-arihant-abode-3bhk-for-sale-in-greater-noida-pppfs\n\n---\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"Coco County Sector 10 Greater Noida West apartment society landscaped gardens residential complex\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"3:4\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 2\\. entity[\"point_of_interest\",\"Coco County\",null,{\"location\":\"Sector 10, Greater Noida West\"}]\n\n# Around ₹1.25 Cr\n\nAn advertised resale example, not a confirmed live offer.\n\n- **3BHK\\:** a listed unit was approximately 1,152 sq ft built-up.\n- **Open space\\:** inspect the actual garden area, tower spacing and apartment's view.\n- **Schools and shopping\\:** compare the route to your preferred school and Gaur City Mall before deciding. citeturn639189search0turn639189search1\n  \n  urlView 3BHK listingshttps://www.nobroker.in/3bhk-flats-for-sale-in-ats-happy-trails-sector-10-noida-prjtl\n\n---\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"ATS Homekraft Happy Trails Sector 10 Greater Noida West residential apartment complex landscaped courtyard\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"3:4\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n## 3\\. entity[\"turn639189business4\",\"ATS HomeKraft Happy Trails\"]\n\n# ₹1.75–2.60 Cr\n\nIndicative advertised 3BHK range; selected configurations and resale prices vary.\n\n- A larger-price-band alternative worth comparing for its residential environment and amenities.\n- **Budget caution\\:** some advertised units may exceed ₹2 Cr.\n- Verify the available unit's usable area, open view, school commute and final all-inclusive cost. citeturn639189search0turn639189search1\n  \n  urlView 3BHK listingshttps://www.nobroker.in/3bhk-flats-for-sale-in-ats-happy-trails-sector-10-greater-noida-prjtl\n\n## Nearby places to check\n\nimage_group{\"query\":[\"St Teresa's School Greater Noida West school building\"],\"aspect_ratio\":\"1:1\",\"layout\":\"carousel\",\"num_per_query\":1}\n\n**entity[\"point_of_interest\",\"St. Teresa's School\",null,{\"location\":\"Greater Noida West\"}]**\n\nA school to investigate near Arihant Abode.             "id": "4c457ce0-ba8d-4f5a-8add-44d01b4079da",
            "author": {

            "create_time": 1791723017.313154,
            "update_time": 1791723023.982214,
            "content": {
                "content_type": "text",
                "parts": [
                    "**Elite X is actually one of the stronger matches for your requirements**, especially because you want a spacious 3BHK with plenty of open space in Sector 10, Greater Noida West.\n\nThe main drawback is the price and possession timeline. It's an under-construction project, so it may not suit you if you want to move in immediately.\n\n<row align=start gap={3}>\n  <AsyncImage query=\"Elite X Elite Group Sector 10 Greater Noida West luxury residential project exterior towers landscaping\" aspectRatio=\"3:4\" width=\"44%\"/>\n  <box flex=\"1\" gap={2}>\n    <title size=\"xl\"><Entity ref=\"turn395319business0\" value=\"ELITE-X\"/></title>\n    <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\">Plot GH-03A, Sector 10, Greater Noida West</text>\n    <title size=\"2xl\">₹2.01–2.10 Cr+</title>\n    <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"xs\">Advertised starting prices vary by listing and price inclusions.</text>\n    <row align=\"center\" gap={2}>\n      <icon name=\"trees\" size=\"lg\" color=\"success\"/>\n      <box flex=\"1\" gap={0}>\n        **69% advertised open area**\n        <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"xs\">Across a 5.44-acre development</text>\n      </box>\n    </row>\n    <row align=\"center\" gap={2}>\n      <icon name=\"ruler\" size=\"lg\"/>\n      <box flex=\"1\" gap={0}>\n        **1,800–2,110 sq ft**\n        <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"xs\">3BHK and 3BHK + study configurations</text>\n      </box>\n    </row>\n    <row align=\"center\" gap={2}>\n      <icon name=\"calendar\" size=\"lg\"/>\n      <box flex=\"1\" gap={0}>\n        **December 2028**\n        <text color=\"secondary\" size=\"xs\">RERA-stated possession timeline</text>\n      </box>\n    </row>\n  </box>\n</row>\n\nThese specifications are reported on the project's website and property listings. The 69% figure is a project marketing claim, so I'd verify the sanctioned layout and how much of that space is actually usable green area. <Cite refs={[\"turn395319search0\",\"turn395319search2\",\"turn395319search3\"]}/>\n\n## Does it meet your requirements?\n\n<box border radius=\"lg\" padding={3} gap={3}>\n  <row align=start gap={2}>\n    <icon name=\"check-circle\" color=\"success\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **Ample open space**\n      <text>Promising on paper, with 69% advertised open area, landscaped spaces and amenities. Ask for the actual master plan before choosing a tower or floor. <Cite refs={[\"turn395319search2\",\"turn395319search3\"]}/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={2}>\n    <icon name=\"check-circle\" color=\"success\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **Schools nearby**\n      <text><Entity ref=\"turn395319business15\" value=\"St. Teresa's School\"/> and <Entity ref=\"turn395319business9\" value=\"Sarvottam International School\"/> are both in Sector 10. Check the driving route from your selected tower. </text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={2}>\n    <icon name=\"check-circle\" color=\"success\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **Shopping nearby**\n      <text>The project advertises its own commercial plaza beside the development. <Entity ref=\"turn395319business2\" value=\"Gaur City Mall\"/> is another established mall in the wider area; check the actual drive time at peak hours. <Cite refs={[\"turn395319search2\",\"turn395319business2\"]}/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n  <divider color=\"subtle\"/>\n  <row align=start gap={2}>\n    <icon name=\"alert-triangle\" color=\"warning\" size=\"xl\"/>\n    <box flex=\"1\" gap={1}>\n      **Budget and possession**\n      <text>New 3BHK listings are around ₹2.01–2.10 crore and above, depending on the listing. Registration, stamp duty and other charges may push the total higher. Possession is expected in 2028, not immediately. <Cite refs={[\"turn395319search0\",\"turn395319search3\",\"turn395319search4\"]}/></text>\n    </box>\n  </row>\n</box>\n\n## My recommendation\n\nIf you're comfortable waiting until 2028 and can stretch your budget beyond ₹2 crore, **keep Elite X at the top of your shortlist**. Its advertised apartment sizes and open-space allocation are a better fit for what you've described than a smaller 3BHK.\n\nBefore paying a booking amount, verify the project on the UP RERA portal using registration number **UPRERAPRJ916631/02/2024**, and obtain a written all-inclusive price quotation.\n\n- [Official Elite X website and floor plans](https://elitex.co/)\n- [Current Elite X resale listings](https://www.nobroker.in/3bhk-flats-for-sale-in-elite-x-sector-10-greater-noida-west-noida-prjtl)\n- [Elite X project details and prices](https://housing.com/in/buy/projects/page/316871-elite-x-by-elite-group-in-noida-extension)\n\nOne question that changes my recommendation: **Is ₹2 crore your absolute all-inclusive limit, or can you stretch to ₹2.15–2.25 crore if the flat and open space are worth it?**"
                ]
            },
